\\\\\\\"因为是一阶相变，autocorrelation/variance 等传统 early-warning signal 在 collapse 前保持有限——无预警\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"定义了 canary 失效的 regime boundary：Class 1 凸反馈系统中传统 canary 理论上无效。我们需证明我们的 canary 在 Class 2+ regime 或非纯反馈场景中有效\\\\\\\"\\\\n    gaps: \\\\\\\"理论框架高度抽象，未给出 LLM 具体场景下的 α/β 估计方法；未测试哪些 canary 在接近一阶相变时仍有部分预警能力\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"entropy\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"autocorrelation\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"variance\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"wang2026contaminated\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"Can Generative Artificial Intelligence Survive Data Contamination? Theoretical Guarantees under Contaminated Recursive Training\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Kevin Wang, Hongqian Niu, Didong Li\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"arXiv (2602.16065)\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2026\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16065\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"core-collapse\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"theoretical-foundation\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"首个不限数据分布假设的正面理论结果：universal approximator 下 contaminated recursive training 仍可收敛\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"收敛速率 = min(baseline 模型收敛速率, 每轮真实数据比例)——真实数据比例是瓶颈\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"突破了先前仅限 discrete/Gaussian 分布的理论框架，适用于一般连续分布\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"为 post-MVP α-sweep 实验提供理论上界：收敛速率由 real data fraction 决定，可预测不同 α 下的 collapse 速率\\\\\\\"\\\\n    gaps: \\\\\\\"universal approximator 假设在实际有限容量模型中不严格成立；未讨论 canary 指标行为\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"convergence rate\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"population risk\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"du2026geometric\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"Escaping Mode Collapse in LLM Generation via Geometric Regulation\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Du, Tanaka-Ishii\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"arXiv (2605.00435)\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2026\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00435\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"core-collapse\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"background\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"将 mode collapse 重新解释为表示空间中的 geometric collapse：生成时模型内部轨迹被限制在低维子空间\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"提出 RMR（Reinforced Mode Regulation），在 value cache 中抑制 dominant self-reinforcing directions\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"针对推理时 mode collapse，非迭代训练场景。但 'geometric collapse = 低维子空间坍缩' 与我们的 effective rank 指标在概念上有连接，可在 related work 中引用以说明 representation-space collapse 检测的跨场景通用性\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"effective rank proxy\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"representation dimensionality\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"gauthier2026alignment\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"Explaining and Preventing Alignment Collapse in Iterative RLHF\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Gauthier, Bach, Jordan\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"arXiv (2605.04266)\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2026\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04266\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"core-collapse\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"complementary\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"迭代 RLHF 中 policy 生成 reward model 的训练数据形成反馈环路，导致 alignment collapse（policy 系统性利用 reward model 盲区）\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"用 Stackelberg game 建模迭代 RLHF 的环路动力学，提出 FPO（foresighted policy optimization）\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"RLHF iterative feedback loop 与本项目 iterative synthetic data loop 结构类似——模型输出成为下一轮输入的反馈环路均导致系统性退化。其 'collapse = 反馈环路中的系统性退化' 叙事支持我们的大框架；Stackelberg game 视角可能启发 canary 的博弈论解释\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"alignment score\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"reward model accuracy\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"shi2026pruning\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"Understanding Performance Collapse in Layer-Pruned LLMs via Decision Representation Transitions\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Shi et al.\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"arXiv (2605.07271)\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2026\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07271\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"core-collapse\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"background\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"层剪枝导致的 performance collapse，定义 Silent Phase（模型预测被单一选项主导 = distributional collapse）和 Decisive Phase\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"剪枝 Silent Phase 立即触发 collapse\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"架构剪枝 collapse，非数据驱动。其 'distributional collapse = 预测分布退化为单峰' 的操作定义与我们 token entropy 下降检测的是同一现象的不同表现\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"prediction distribution entropy\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"task accuracy\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"tubingen2026diffusion\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"Layer Collapse in Diffusion Language Models\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Tübingen AI Center et al.\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"arXiv (2605.06366)\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2026\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06366\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"core-collapse\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"background\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"LLaDA-8B 中发现层级 activation collapse：少数早期层的 activation 被单个 super-outlier 主导\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"剪掉此 outlier 导致模型输出退化为重复随机 token\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"Diffusion LM 的架构 collapse，与迭代合成数据训练无关。但 activation collapse 的表征与 spectral collapse 有交集\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"activation magnitude\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"output repetition\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  # ============================================================\\\\n  # CANARY / EARLY WARNING METRICS\\\\n  # ============================================================\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"chen2025scales\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"Characterizing Model Behavior Under Synthetic Data Training: An Empirical Study Across Scales and Mixing Ratios\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Anonymous\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"arXiv (2510.05133)\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2025\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.05133\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"canary-metrics\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"direct-competitor\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"**Calibration degradation precedes accuracy loss** — ECE 是 collapse 的 early warning signal\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"20% synthetic data 以下性能稳定，30% 以上退化加速\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"大模型（6.9B-12B）比小模型（410M-1.4B）更鲁棒\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"推理任务比检索任务更易退化\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"iteration 1 + 30% synthetic 时 ECE 从 0.08 升至 0.14（+75%）\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"**最直接的竞争者/先驱**。证实 ECE 可作为 canary，但未做 lead-lag 分析，未建立多指标 panel 框架\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"ECE\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"accuracy\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"perplexity\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"gambetta2024surplexity\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"Learning by Surprise: Surplexity for Mitigating Model Collapse in Generative AI\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Daniele Gambetta et al.\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"arXiv (2410.12341)\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2024\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12341\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"canary-metrics\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"direct-competitor\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"提出 surplexity（model surprise 度量）同时用于检测 collapse 和过滤训练数据\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"用 semantic networks 分析文本重复性和语义多样性\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"用 next-token probability 定量衡量 diversity loss\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"高 surplexity 过滤策略至少与 human-data baseline 同效，且更能降低分布偏斜\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"不需要区分 human vs AI-generated data\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"surplexity 可作为我们 canary 指标候选之一；其 semantic network 方法提供了 diversity 度量的另一视角\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"surplexity\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"semantic network metrics\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"next-token probability\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"qiu2025spectral\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"Spectral Alignment as Predictor of Loss Explosion in Neural Network Training\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Haiquan Qiu, You Wu, Yingjie Tan, Yaqing Wang, Quanming Yao\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"arXiv (2510.04202)\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2025\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04202\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"canary-metrics\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"method-component\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"Spectral Alignment (SA) 监控层输入与权重矩阵主奇异向量的分布对齐\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"SA sign diversity collapse 是 representational collapse 和训练发散的早期预测器\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"比 weight norm、gradient norm 等传统指标更早、更清晰地检测到训练失败\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"健康层的 SA 应有正负均衡分布，collapse 到单侧则预示不稳定\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"SA 是潜在的高价值 canary 指标——专门设计为 early warning，可直接纳入我们的指标 panel\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"spectral alignment\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"weight norm\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"gradient norm\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"he2025closer\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"A Closer Look at Model Collapse: From a Generalization-to-Memorization Perspective\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Shiliang He et al.\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"NeurIPS 2025 (Spotlight)\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2025\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16499\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"canary-metrics\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"direct-competitor\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"识别 diffusion model collapse 中的 generalization → memorization 转变\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"**训练数据的 entropy 递减是 collapse 的直接驱动因素和清晰指标**\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"提出基于 entropy 的数据选择策略，有效缓解 collapse\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"data entropy 作为 canary 的有效性已被验证（diffusion domain）；需测试是否在 LLM 场景同样有效\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"entropy\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"FID\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"diversity\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"ba2024fill\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"Fill In The Gaps: Model Calibration and Generalization with Synthetic Data\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Yang Ba, Michelle V Mancenido, Rong Pan\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"EMNLP 2024\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2024\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10864\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"canary-metrics\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"background\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"用 LLM 生成合成数据来修复 calibration 错误的 reliability diagram bins\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"推导了基于 PAC 框架的 ECE bound\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"合成数据可改善 calibration 但需精心设计生成策略\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"ECE 的理论 bound 可为我们的 canary 阈值设定提供理论依据\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"ECE\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"accuracy\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"sigma2026spectral\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"SIGMA: Scalable Spectral Insights for LLM Model Collapse\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Yi Gu, Lingyou Pang, Xiangkun Ye, Tianyu Wang, Jianyu Lin, Carey E. Priebe, Alexander Aue\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"arXiv (2601.03385)\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2026\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03385\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"canary-metrics\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"direct-competitor\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"基于 embedding Gram matrix 谱分析的 collapse 监控框架：Track I（确定性谱界）保守但鲁棒，Track II（随机谱缩放）灵敏但需采样\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"Track I/II 的分歧本身是 early-stage collapse 的诊断信号，早于表面重复性伪影\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"随机谱界使部分观测的 Gram matrix 也可用于大规模模型的可扩展监控\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"最直接的 spectral canary 竞争者。我们的 effective rank 是其简化版（单一标量 vs 完整谱界）；需在 related work 中明确差异：SIGMA 提供双轨谱界，我们用单一 effective rank 换取更低计算开销和多指标 panel 集成\\\\\\\"\\\\n    gaps: \\\\\\\"未做 lead-lag 分析，未与 ECE/diversity 等非谱指标对比；Track I/II 的计算开销在 iterative loop 多轮场景下可能不可忽略\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"Gram matrix eigenspectrum\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"spectral norm bounds\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"kalinowski2026topology\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"Monitoring Neural Training with Topology: A Footprint-Predictable Collapse Index\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Alexander Kalinowski\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"arXiv (2604.26984)\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2026\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26984\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"canary-metrics\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"background\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"提出基于 Modular Morse Homology Maintenance (MMHM) 的在线拓扑监控方案，增量更新避免每轮重建\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"Collapse Index (CI) 组合多尺度拓扑特征，在 LLM fine-tuning 和 temporal KGE 训练中提供低延迟 early-warning\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"拓扑方法捕捉表示空间的各向异性和多尺度结构丧失\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"topology-based monitoring 是与 spectral 方法正交的 collapse 检测路线。我们排除了该方法（计算开销高、可解释性弱），但 related work 需要对比说明选择 spectral/calibration panel 而非 topology 的理由\\\\\\\"\\\\n    gaps: \\\\\\\"计算开销显著高于 effective rank 等标量指标；未在 iterative synthetic training loop 场景下测试；与 ECE 等轻量 canary 无对比\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"Collapse Index\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"persistent homology features\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"bergsma2026scaling\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"Scaling with Collapse: Efficient and Predictable Training of LLM Families\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Bergsma et al.\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"arXiv (2509.25087, rev. Mar 2026)\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2026\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25087\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"canary-metrics\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"background\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"'Collapse' 此处指 loss curve 归一化后坍缩到通用轨迹（非 model collapse）\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"deviation-from-collapse 作为训练病理的早期诊断信号\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"不同语境下的 'collapse'。但其 '偏离通用轨迹 = 训练出问题' 的监控思路与我们的 canary 理念相似——均从时序异常检测角度切入\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"loss curve deviation\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"scaling law exponents\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  # ============================================================\\\\n  # DIVERSITY METRICS (BACKGROUND)\\\\n  # ============================================================\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"zhu2018texygen\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"Texygen: A Benchmarking Platform for Text Generation Models\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Yaoming Zhu et al.\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"SIGIR 2018\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2018\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/1802.01886\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"canary-metrics\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"background\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"提出 Self-BLEU 度量：计算生成句子间的 BLEU 相似度，Self-BLEU 越高多样性越低\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"成为文本生成多样性评估的标准基准之一\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"Self-BLEU 是我们 diversity canary 的经典候选\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"Self-BLEU\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"BLEU\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"li2016diversity\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"A Diversity-Promoting Objective Function for Neural Conversation Models\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Jiwei Li et al.\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"NAACL 2016\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2016\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/1510.03055\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"canary-metrics\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"background\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"提出 distinct-1 / distinct-2 指标衡量生成文本的 n-gram 多样性\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"计算 unique n-gram 数占总 n-gram 数的比例\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"distinct-n 是最廉价的 diversity canary 之一，计算开销极低\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"distinct-1\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"distinct-2\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  # ============================================================\\\\n  # REPRESENTATION / SPECTRAL COLLAPSE\\\\n  # ============================================================\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"jing2022understanding\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"Understanding Dimensional Collapse in Contrastive Self-supervised Learning\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Li Jing et al.\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"ICLR 2022\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2022\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://openreview.net/forum?id=YevsQ05DEN7\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"canary-metrics\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"method-component\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"定义 dimensional collapse：embedding 向量只跨越低维子空间\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"effective rank（基于归一化特征值 entropy 的指数）作为 collapse 度量\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"strong augmentation 和 implicit regularization 驱动低秩解\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"effective rank 的定义和计算方式可直接迁移到我们的 embedding 空间监控\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"effective rank\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"singular value spectrum\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"noci2024mind\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"Mind the Gap: A Spectral Analysis of Rank Collapse and Signal Propagation in Transformers\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Lorenzo Noci et al.\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"NeurIPS 2024\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2024\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07799\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"canary-metrics\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"method-component\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"用 Random Matrix Theory 分析 attention 层的 rank collapse\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"发现 attention matrix 最大两个奇异值之间的 spectral gap 是 width-collapse 的根因\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"rank collapse in depth: 所有 token 沿层收敛到单一表示\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"spectral gap 作为另一种 spectral canary 候选；其 RMT 分析框架可启发我们的理论工作\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"spectral gap\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"singular values\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"effective rank\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  # ============================================================\\\\n  # DATA MIXING & ACCUMULATION\\\\n  # ============================================================\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"kazdan2025collapse\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"Collapse or Thrive? Perils and Promises of Synthetic Data in a Self-Generating World\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Joshua Kazdan, Rylan Schaeffer, Apratim Dey, Matthias Gerstgrasser, Rafael Rafailov, David L. Donoho, Sanmi Koyejo\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"ICML 2025\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2025\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.16713\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"data-mixing\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"foundation\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"系统对比 replace vs accumulate 场景\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"replace → collapse；accumulate → 可避免 collapse\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"发现 real data 和 synthetic data 之间存在非平凡的交互效应\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"synthetic data 的价值取决于 real data 的绝对数量\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"直接对应我们的 alpha（mixing ratio）实验维度；其 replace vs accumulate 框架是我们实验设计的重要参考\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"test loss\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"dey2024breaking\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"Is Model Collapse Inevitable? Breaking the Curse of Recursion by Accumulating Real and Synthetic Data\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Apratim Dey, David Donoho, et al.\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"ICML 2024\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2024\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.01413\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"data-mixing\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"foundation\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"替换数据 → 发散 population risk；累积数据 → 避免发散\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"证明累积策略在多种模型（GMM, HMM, LLM）上有效\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"为我们的 alpha 实验提供理论预测：累积 vs 替换是 collapse 速率的关键调节因子\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"population risk\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"epstein2024howbad\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"How Bad is Training on Synthetic Data? A Statistical Analysis of Language Model Collapse\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Mohamed El Amine Epstein et al.\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"arXiv (2404.05090)\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2024\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.05090\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"data-mixing\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"background\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"纯合成数据训练不可避免 collapse\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"给出 synthetic data 比例上界的估计——低于此比例可避免 collapse\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"递归训练使原始分布尾部消失\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"其 mixing ratio 上界估计可对比我们实验中观测到的 canary 触发阈值\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"perplexity\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"fu2025selfconsuming\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"A Theoretical Perspective: How to Prevent Model Collapse in Self-consuming Training Loops\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Shi Fu, Yingjie Wang, Yuzhu Chen, Xinmei Tian, Dacheng Tao\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"ICLR 2025\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2025\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.18865\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"data-mixing\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"theoretical-foundation\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"引入 recursive stability 概念，首次给出 self-consuming loop 的泛化分析\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"恒定比例的真实数据即可保证 STL 收敛；同时给出最优合成数据量的 insight\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"模型架构和 real/synthetic 数据比例共同决定 STL 的成败\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"为 post-MVP α-sweep（synthetic ratio 梯度）实验提供理论预期：50% accumulation 条件下收敛有理论保证\\\\\\\"\\\\n    gaps: \\\\\\\"理论分析限于特定 generalization bound 框架，未涉及 canary 指标的行为；未讨论不同 α 下 collapse 速率的精细结构\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"generalization bound\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"population risk\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"bakshi2026improvement\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"From Collapse to Improvement: Statistical Perspectives on the Evolutionary Dynamics of Iterative Training on Contaminated Sources\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Soham Bakshi, Sunrit Chakraborty\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"arXiv (2602.10531)\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2026\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10531\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"data-mixing\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"theoretical-foundation\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"αt（每轮混合权重）trajectory 和 nt（每轮样本量）scaling 的交互决定三种 regime：collapse / improvement / consistency\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"当 αt 衰减足够快且 nt 增长足够快时，迭代估计可收敛到真实分布（improvement regime）\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"αt 衰减不足或 nt 增长不足则进入 collapse regime；边界条件给出 consistency regime\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"在 next-token prediction 语言模型上分析完整迭代演化动态\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"αt/nt 条件直接对应我们 α-sweep 实验的理论预期：不同 synthetic ratio 衰减曲线下 canary 应呈现不同时序模式\\\\\\\"\\\\n    gaps: \\\\\\\"理论结果基于渐近分析，有限代数下的 transient behavior 未充分刻画；未讨论哪些指标能区分三种 regime\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"population risk\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"total variation distance\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"he2025golden\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"Golden Ratio Weighting Prevents Model Collapse\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Shiliang He, Zhongbin Xu, Yan Cheng\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"arXiv (2502.18049)\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2025\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.18049\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"data-mixing\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"theoretical-foundation\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"最优 real data 权重在 Gaussian estimation、GLM、非参数估计中均收敛到黄金比例倒数 1/φ ≈ 0.618\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"最优权重 w*(λ) 作为正则化参数的函数既非单调也非凸——risk landscape 在 mixing ratio 空间上本身不是单调的\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"提供了 mixing ratio 非单调性的首个理论框架\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"我们在 α-sweep 中观测到的非单调 dose-response（α=0.25 比 α=0.50 collapse 更严重）可视为该理论在 diversity metrics 上的实证验证。论文 Discussion 的理论锚点。\\\\\\\"\\\\n    gaps: \\\\\\\"理论基于线性回归和高斯分布，未直接推广到 LLM diversity metrics\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"population risk\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"optimal weight\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"xu2025mixing\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"Preventing Model Collapse Under Overparametrization: Optimal Mixing Ratios\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Zhongbin Xu, Yan Cheng\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"arXiv (2509.22341)\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2025\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22341\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"data-mixing\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"theoretical-foundation\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"过参数化 regime（插值学习 + ridge 回归）中分析最优混合比例\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"插值学习：最优 real data 比例收敛到 1/φ\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"Ridge 回归：最优比例受谱几何控制，至少为 1/2\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"w > 1/3 时 variance 随迭代单调递减；w < 1/3 时 variance 可能增长——存在临界阈值\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"1/3 临界阈值与我们 α=0.25（real weight=0.75 vs synthetic weight=0.25）处出现最强 collapse 的观测有概念对应。不同 regime 下 variance 行为质变支持我们的 regime-dependent 叙事。\\\\\\\"\\\\n    gaps: \\\\\\\"参数空间不完全对齐（他们的 w 是 real data weight，我们的 α 是 synthetic ratio）；未讨论 diversity metrics\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"variance\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"population risk\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"optimal mixing ratio\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  # ============================================================\\\\n  # MITIGATION & VERIFICATION\\\\n  # ============================================================\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"feng2025escaping\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"Escaping Model Collapse via Synthetic Data Verification\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Yunzhen Feng et al.\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"NeurIPS 2025\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2025\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.16657\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"data-mixing\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"background\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"通过外部 verifier（human 或 better model）注入信息可避免 collapse\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"verifier-guided retraining 带来短期改善，但最终收敛到 verifier 的 knowledge center\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"除非 verifier 完全可靠，早期收益会 plateau 甚至逆转\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"verification 作为 intervention 策略——如果 canary 触发，可考虑此类 reactive intervention\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"test loss\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"barzilai2025consistency\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"When Models Don't Collapse: On the Consistency of Iterative MLE\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Daniel Barzilai, Ohad Shamir\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"ICML 2025\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2025\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19046\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"core-collapse\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"background\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"在标准 MLE 一致性假设下，即使 real data fraction 趋近零也可避免 collapse\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"但证明了某些情况下 collapse 可以任意快地发生——首次给出严格 collapse 反例\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"理论上的 collapse 可发生任意快的结论强化了 early warning 的价值\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: []\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"dces2026counter\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"Countering Model Collapse in Iterative Self-Training via Dynamic Center-Edge Sampling\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Anonymous\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"MDPI Electronics (2026)\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2026\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://www.mdpi.com/2079-9292/15/4/869\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"data-mixing\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"background\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"提出 DCES（dynamic center-edge sampling）数据选择框架\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"DCES 在 ECE（0.1058 vs 0.1200）和 entropy 保持上优于随机选择\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"其 ECE 和 entropy 对比数据为我们的指标 baseline 提供参考\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"ECE\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"entropy\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  # ============================================================\\\\n  # POSITION / SURVEY / EPISTEMICS\\\\n  # ============================================================\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"schaeffer2025position\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"Position: Model Collapse Does Not Mean What You Think\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Rylan Schaeffer, Joshua Kazdan, Alvan Caleb Arulandu, Sanmi Koyejo\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"arXiv (2503.03150)\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2025\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.03150\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"core-collapse\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"background\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"文献中实际包含 8 种不同且有时矛盾的 model collapse 定义\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"许多 collapse 预测依赖于与现实不符的假设\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"多种 collapse 场景是可以轻易避免的\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"提醒我们明确定义 collapse 的操作标准；不同定义可能需要不同的 canary 指标\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: []\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"wang2025epistemic\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"Epistemic Diversity and Knowledge Collapse in Large Language Models\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Wang et al.\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"arXiv (2510.04226)\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2025\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04226\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"core-collapse\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"background\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"27 个 LLM 在 155 个主题上的 epistemic diversity 评估\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"所有模型的 epistemic diversity 低于基本 web 搜索\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"模型越大 epistemic diversity 反而越低；RAG 有正面效果\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"knowledge collapse 维度可扩展我们对 diversity canary 的理解——不仅是 lexical/syntactic 还有 epistemic\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\\\\\"epistemic diversity\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n  # ============================================================\\\\n  # METHODOLOGY (GRANGER / LEAD-LAG)\\\\n  # ============================================================\\\\n  - Fox\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"Annual Review of Statistics and Its Application\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2022\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2105.02675\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"methodology\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"method-component\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"Granger causality 综述：从经典线性到非线性/neural 方法\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"Panel data 场景的 Granger causality 扩展\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"VAR 和 neural Granger causality 的对比\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"为我们的 panel Granger causality 分析提供方法论基础\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: []\\\\n\\\\n  - id: \\\\\\\"marcinkevics2022neural\\\\\\\"\\\\n    title: \\\\\\\"Granger Causality Using Neural Networks\\\\\\\"\\\\n    authors: \\\\\\\"Raphael Marcinkevics, Julia Vogt\\\\\\\"\\\\n    venue: \\\\\\\"arXiv (2208.03703)\\\\\\\"\\\\n    year: 2022\\\\n    url: \\\\\\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2208.03703\\\\\\\"\\\\n    category: \\\\\\\"methodology\\\\\\\"\\\\n    role: \\\\\\\"method-component\\\\\\\"\\\\n    key_findings:\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"用 neural network 估计 Granger causality，突破线性假设\\\\\\\"\\\\n      - \\\\\\\"两个模型对比法：含/不含外生变量的预测误差差异即 Granger causality 证据\\\\\\\"\\\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\\\\\"我们的多指标时序可能存在非线性关系，neural Granger causality 是自然选择\\\\\\\"\\\\n    metrics_studied: []\\\\n\\\\ngaps:\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"无人系统做过 canary 指标的 lead-lag / Granger causality 分析——现有工作只在事后观察到指标变化，未量化哪些指标能提前多少步预测 collapse\\\\\\\"\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"ECE 在 collapse 场景下几乎未被系统研究——chen2025scales 是唯一报告 ECE 先于 accuracy 退化的工作，但未做因果分析\\\\\\\"\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"embedding effective rank 未在 iterative synthetic training 场景下被测试——仅在 contrastive learning/transformer 训练中研究过\\\\\\\"\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"多指标 panel 框架缺失——无人同时追踪 ECE + effective rank + diversity + perplexity 的时序行为并分析其 lead-lag 关系\\\\\\\"\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"alpha（synthetic ratio）对 canary 灵敏度的影响未被研究——现有工作只关注 alpha 对最终性能的影响，不关注 canary 的 lead time 如何随 alpha 变化\\\\\\\"\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"diversity dose-response 非单调性（α=0.25 比 α=0.50 collapse 更严重）无先例报告——He et al. 2025 从理论上预测 risk landscape 非单调，但无人在 LLM diversity 上实证验证\\\\\\\"\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"predictive intervention vs reactive intervention 缺少对比——没有工作量化 early warning 带来的实际干预收益\\\\\\\"\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"surplexity 和 spectral alignment 未在 iterative collapse 场景下交叉验证\\\\\\\"\\\\n  - \\\\\\\"跨任务 canary 普适性未知——reasoning vs retrieval vs creative tasks 中哪些 canary 更可靠\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nnovelty_assessment: |\\\\n  我们项目的核心 novelty 在于：\\\\n\\\\n  1. **Panel Granger Causality 框架**：首次将多指标时序数据的因果分析方法应用于 model collapse 早期预警。\\\\n     现有工作（chen2025scales, gambetta2024surplexity）只是事后观察到某些指标先变化，\\\\n     但没有人做过系统的 lead-lag 量化分析。\\\\n\\\\n  2. **多指标 Canary Panel**：同时追踪 ECE、effective rank、Self-BLEU/distinct-n、\\\\n     perplexity、entropy、surplexity 等指标，分析其相互关系和各自的 lead time。\\\\n     现有工作都是单一指标或最多两三个指标的孤立分析。\\\\n\\\\n  3. **alpha-dependent Canary Sensitivity**：系统研究 synthetic ratio 如何影响各 canary\\\\n     指标的灵敏度和 lead time。dohmatob2025strong 证明极小的 alpha 就能触发 collapse，\\\\n     但没人研究 canary 在不同 alpha 下的行为。\\\\n\\\\n  4. **与已有工作的差异**：\\\\n     - vs chen2025scales: 他们发现 ECE 先退化但没有做因果分析；我们用 Granger causality 量化\\\\n     - vs gambetta2024surplexity: 他们提出 surplexity 用于检测和缓解 collapse；\\\\n       我们将其纳入更广的 panel 并研究其 lead-lag 特性\\\\n     - vs he2025closer: 他们在 diffusion 中用 entropy 作为指标；我们在 LLM 中测试更多指标\\\\n     - vs qiu2025spectral: 他们的 SA 用于训练 loss explosion 预警；\\\\n       我们将类似思路迁移到 iterative collapse 场景\\\\n\\\\n  5. **潜在风险**：chen2025scales 已经非常接近我们的方向。如果他们扩展到 lead-lag 分析，\\\\n     我们的 novelty 会被削弱。需要快速推进并在方法论（Granger causality）上建立壁垒。\\\\n\\\", \\\"total_chars\\\": 29202, \\\"truncated\\\": false}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {}}\"}",

id: \\\"shumailov2024curse\\\"\\n    title: \\\"The Curse of Recursion: Training on Generated Data Makes Models Forget\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Ilia Shumailov, Zakhar Shumaylov, Yiren Zhao, Yarin Gal, Nicolas Papernot, Ross Anderson\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"Nature\\\"\\n    year: 2024\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.17493\\\"\\n    category: \\\"core-collapse\\\"\\n    role: \\\"foundation\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"定义 model collapse 现象：迭代用合成数据替换真实数据训练会导致分布尾部消失\\\"\\n      - \\\"区分 early model collapse（尾部信息丢失）和 late model collapse（分布完全收敛）\\\"\\n      - \\\"三类误差来源：statistical approximation, functional expressivity, functional approximation，跨代复合\\\"\\n      - \\\"在 VAE、GMM、LLM 上均验证了 collapse 的普遍性\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"奠基论文，定义了我们研究的核心现象。我们的 canary 指标旨在提前检测其描述的 early collapse 阶段\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"perplexity\\\", \\\"distributional tail mass\\\"]\\n\\n  - \\\"提出 Model Autophagy Disorder (MAD) 概念\\\"\\n      - \\\"autophagous loop 中没有足够新鲜真实数据时，质量或多样性必然渐进下降\\\"\\n      - \\\"在图像生成领域验证 self-consuming loop 的退化效应\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"MAD 框架与我们的 iterative loop 设置直接对应；其 quality vs diversity tradeoff 可能反映在不同 canary 指标上\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"FID\\\", \\\"precision\\\", \\\"recall\\\", \\\"diversity\\\"]\\n\\n  - \\\"首次系统研究 LLM 的 self-consuming training loop\\\"\\n      - \\\"full synthetic cycle（完全替换）导致单点 collapse；data augmentation cycle 导致 diversity 缓慢下降\\\"\\n      - \\\"expanding data cycle（累积）在 50 代内保持 diversity\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"其不同 data cycle 策略的对比直接对应我们的 alpha（synthetic ratio）实验设置\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"diversity\\\", \\\"quality\\\"]\\n\\n  - id: \\\"truong2025entropy\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Entropy Collapse: A Universal Failure Mode of Intelligent Systems\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Truong Xuan Khanh, Truong Quynh Hoa\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv (2512.12381)\\\"\\n    year: 2025\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.12381\\\"\\n    category: \\\"core-collapse\\\"\\n    role: \\\"theoretical-foundation\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"证明 entropy collapse 是一阶（不连续）相变，临界点 α_c(β) = 1/(1−β)，entropy 在临界点跳变 0.698 nats\\\"\\n      - \\\"两个 universality class：Class 1（凸反馈，如 power-law）不可逆 ν=1；Class 2（线性反馈）可逆 ν=1/2\\\"\\n      - \\\"因为是一阶相变，autocorrelation/variance 等传统 early-warning signal 在 collapse 前保持有限——无预警\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"定义了 canary 失效的 regime boundary：Class 1 凸反馈系统中传统 canary 理论上无效。我们需证明我们的 canary 在 Class 2+ regime 或非纯反馈场景中有效\\\"\\n    gaps: \\\"理论框架高度抽象，未给出 LLM 具体场景下的 α/β 估计方法；未测试哪些 canary 在接近一阶相变时仍有部分预警能力\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"entropy\\\", \\\"autocorrelation\\\", \\\"variance\\\"]\\n\\n  - id: \\\"wang2026contaminated\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Can Generative Artificial Intelligence Survive Data Contamination? Theoretical Guarantees under Contaminated Recursive Training\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Kevin Wang, Hongqian Niu, Didong Li\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv (2602.16065)\\\"\\n    year: 2026\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16065\\\"\\n    category: \\\"core-collapse\\\"\\n    role: \\\"theoretical-foundation\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"首个不限数据分布假设的正面理论结果：universal approximator 下 contaminated recursive training 仍可收敛\\\"\\n      - \\\"收敛速率 = min(baseline 模型收敛速率, 每轮真实数据比例)——真实数据比例是瓶颈\\\"\\n      - \\\"突破了先前仅限 discrete/Gaussian 分布的理论框架，适用于一般连续分布\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"为 post-MVP α-sweep 实验提供理论上界：收敛速率由 real data fraction 决定，可预测不同 α 下的 collapse 速率\\\"\\n    gaps: \\\"universal approximator 假设在实际有限容量模型中不严格成立；未讨论 canary 指标行为\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"convergence rate\\\", \\\"population risk\\\"]\\n\\n  - id: \\\"du2026geometric\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Escaping Mode Collapse in LLM Generation via Geometric Regulation\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Du, Tanaka-Ishii\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv (2605.00435)\\\"\\n    year: 2026\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00435\\\"\\n    category: \\\"core-collapse\\\"\\n    role: \\\"background\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"将 mode collapse 重新解释为表示空间中的 geometric collapse：生成时模型内部轨迹被限制在低维子空间\\\"\\n      - \\\"提出 RMR（Reinforced Mode Regulation），在 value cache 中抑制 dominant self-reinforcing directions\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"针对推理时 mode collapse，非迭代训练场景。但 'geometric collapse = 低维子空间坍缩' 与我们的 effective rank 指标在概念上有连接，可在 related work 中引用以说明 representation-space collapse 检测的跨场景通用性\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"effective rank proxy\\\", \\\"representation dimensionality\\\"]\\n\\n  - id: \\\"gauthier2026alignment\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Explaining and Preventing Alignment Collapse in Iterative RLHF\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Gauthier, Bach, Jordan\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv (2605.04266)\\\"\\n    year: 2026\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04266\\\"\\n    category: \\\"core-collapse\\\"\\n    role: \\\"complementary\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"迭代 RLHF 中 policy 生成 reward model 的训练数据形成反馈环路，导致 alignment collapse（policy 系统性利用 reward model 盲区）\\\"\\n      - \\\"用 Stackelberg game 建模迭代 RLHF 的环路动力学，提出 FPO（foresighted policy optimization）\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"RLHF iterative feedback loop 与本项目 iterative synthetic data loop 结构类似——模型输出成为下一轮输入的反馈环路均导致系统性退化。其 'collapse = 反馈环路中的系统性退化' 叙事支持我们的大框架；Stackelberg game 视角可能启发 canary 的博弈论解释\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"alignment score\\\", \\\"reward model accuracy\\\"]\\n\\n  - id: \\\"shi2026pruning\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Understanding Performance Collapse in Layer-Pruned LLMs via Decision Representation Transitions\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Shi et al.\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv (2605.07271)\\\"\\n    year: 2026\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07271\\\"\\n    category: \\\"core-collapse\\\"\\n    role: \\\"background\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"层剪枝导致的 performance collapse，定义 Silent Phase（模型预测被单一选项主导 = distributional collapse）和 Decisive Phase\\\"\\n      - \\\"剪枝 Silent Phase 立即触发 collapse\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"架构剪枝 collapse，非数据驱动。其 'distributional collapse = 预测分布退化为单峰' 的操作定义与我们 token entropy 下降检测的是同一现象的不同表现\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"prediction distribution entropy\\\", \\\"task accuracy\\\"]\\n\\n  - id: \\\"tubingen2026diffusion\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Layer Collapse in Diffusion Language Models\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Tübingen AI Center et al.\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv (2605.06366)\\\"\\n    year: 2026\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06366\\\"\\n    category: \\\"core-collapse\\\"\\n    role: \\\"background\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"LLaDA-8B 中发现层级 activation collapse：少数早期层的 activation 被单个 super-outlier 主导\\\"\\n      - \\\"剪掉此 outlier 导致模型输出退化为重复随机 token\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"Diffusion LM 的架构 collapse，与迭代合成数据训练无关。但 activation collapse 的表征与 spectral collapse 有交集\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"activation magnitude\\\", \\\"output repetition\\\"]\\n\\n  # ============================================================\\n  # CANARY / EARLY WARNING METRICS\\n  # ============================================================\\n  - id: \\\"chen2025scales\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Characterizing Model Behavior Under Synthetic Data Training: An Empirical Study Across Scales and Mixing Ratios\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Anonymous\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv (2510.05133)\\\"\\n    year: 2025\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.05133\\\"\\n    category: \\\"canary-metrics\\\"\\n    role: \\\"direct-competitor\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"**Calibration degradation precedes accuracy loss** — ECE 是 collapse 的 early warning signal\\\"\\n      - \\\"20% synthetic data 以下性能稳定，30% 以上退化加速\\\"\\n      - \\\"大模型（6.9B-12B）比小模型（410M-1.4B）更鲁棒\\\"\\n      - \\\"推理任务比检索任务更易退化\\\"\\n      - \\\"iteration 1 + 30% synthetic 时 ECE 从 0.08 升至 0.14（+75%）\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"**最直接的竞争者/先驱**。证实 ECE 可作为 canary，但未做 lead-lag 分析，未建立多指标 panel 框架\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"ECE\\\", \\\"accuracy\\\", \\\"perplexity\\\"]\\n\\n  - id: \\\"gambetta2024surplexity\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Learning by Surprise: Surplexity for Mitigating Model Collapse in Generative AI\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Daniele Gambetta et al.\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv (2410.12341)\\\"\\n    year: 2024\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12341\\\"\\n    category: \\\"canary-metrics\\\"\\n    role: \\\"direct-competitor\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"提出 surplexity（model surprise 度量）同时用于检测 collapse 和过滤训练数据\\\"\\n      - \\\"用 semantic networks 分析文本重复性和语义多样性\\\"\\n      - \\\"用 next-token probability 定量衡量 diversity loss\\\"\\n      - \\\"高 surplexity 过滤策略至少与 human-data baseline 同效，且更能降低分布偏斜\\\"\\n      - \\\"不需要区分 human vs AI-generated data\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"surplexity 可作为我们 canary 指标候选之一；其 semantic network 方法提供了 diversity 度量的另一视角\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"surplexity\\\", \\\"semantic network metrics\\\", \\\"next-token probability\\\"]\\n\\n  - id: \\\"qiu2025spectral\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Spectral Alignment as Predictor of Loss Explosion in Neural Network Training\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Haiquan Qiu, You Wu, Yingjie Tan, Yaqing Wang, Quanming Yao\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv (2510.04202)\\\"\\n    year: 2025\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04202\\\"\\n    category: \\\"canary-metrics\\\"\\n    role: \\\"method-component\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"Spectral Alignment (SA) 监控层输入与权重矩阵主奇异向量的分布对齐\\\"\\n      - \\\"SA sign diversity collapse 是 representational collapse 和训练发散的早期预测器\\\"\\n      - \\\"比 weight norm、gradient norm 等传统指标更早、更清晰地检测到训练失败\\\"\\n      - \\\"健康层的 SA 应有正负均衡分布，collapse 到单侧则预示不稳定\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"SA 是潜在的高价值 canary 指标——专门设计为 early warning，可直接纳入我们的指标 panel\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"spectral alignment\\\", \\\"weight norm\\\", \\\"gradient norm\\\"]\\n\\n  - id: \\\"he2025closer\\\"\\n    title: \\\"A Closer Look at Model Collapse: From a Generalization-to-Memorization Perspective\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Shiliang He et al.\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"NeurIPS 2025 (Spotlight)\\\"\\n    year: 2025\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16499\\\"\\n    category: \\\"canary-metrics\\\"\\n    role: \\\"direct-competitor\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"识别 diffusion model collapse 中的 generalization → memorization 转变\\\"\\n      - \\\"**训练数据的 entropy 递减是 collapse 的直接驱动因素和清晰指标**\\\"\\n      - \\\"提出基于 entropy 的数据选择策略，有效缓解 collapse\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"data entropy 作为 canary 的有效性已被验证（diffusion domain）；需测试是否在 LLM 场景同样有效\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"entropy\\\", \\\"FID\\\", \\\"diversity\\\"]\\n\\n  - id: \\\"ba2024fill\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Fill In The Gaps: Model Calibration and Generalization with Synthetic Data\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Yang Ba, Michelle V Mancenido, Rong Pan\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"EMNLP 2024\\\"\\n    year: 2024\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10864\\\"\\n    category: \\\"canary-metrics\\\"\\n    role: \\\"background\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"用 LLM 生成合成数据来修复 calibration 错误的 reliability diagram bins\\\"\\n      - \\\"推导了基于 PAC 框架的 ECE bound\\\"\\n      - \\\"合成数据可改善 calibration 但需精心设计生成策略\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"ECE 的理论 bound 可为我们的 canary 阈值设定提供理论依据\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"ECE\\\", \\\"accuracy\\\"]\\n\\n  - id: \\\"sigma2026spectral\\\"\\n    title: \\\"SIGMA: Scalable Spectral Insights for LLM Model Collapse\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Yi Gu, Lingyou Pang, Xiangkun Ye, Tianyu Wang, Jianyu Lin, Carey E. Priebe, Alexander Aue\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv (2601.03385)\\\"\\n    year: 2026\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03385\\\"\\n    category: \\\"canary-metrics\\\"\\n    role: \\\"direct-competitor\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"基于 embedding Gram matrix 谱分析的 collapse 监控框架：Track I（确定性谱界）保守但鲁棒，Track II（随机谱缩放）灵敏但需采样\\\"\\n      - \\\"Track I/II 的分歧本身是 early-stage collapse 的诊断信号，早于表面重复性伪影\\\"\\n      - \\\"随机谱界使部分观测的 Gram matrix 也可用于大规模模型的可扩展监控\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"最直接的 spectral canary 竞争者。我们的 effective rank 是其简化版（单一标量 vs 完整谱界）；需在 related work 中明确差异：SIGMA 提供双轨谱界，我们用单一 effective rank 换取更低计算开销和多指标 panel 集成\\\"\\n    gaps: \\\"未做 lead-lag 分析，未与 ECE/diversity 等非谱指标对比；Track I/II 的计算开销在 iterative loop 多轮场景下可能不可忽略\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"Gram matrix eigenspectrum\\\", \\\"spectral norm bounds\\\"]\\n\\n  - id: \\\"kalinowski2026topology\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Monitoring Neural Training with Topology: A Footprint-Predictable Collapse Index\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Alexander Kalinowski\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv (2604.26984)\\\"\\n    year: 2026\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26984\\\"\\n    category: \\\"canary-metrics\\\"\\n    role: \\\"background\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"提出基于 Modular Morse Homology Maintenance (MMHM) 的在线拓扑监控方案，增量更新避免每轮重建\\\"\\n      - \\\"Collapse Index (CI) 组合多尺度拓扑特征，在 LLM fine-tuning 和 temporal KGE 训练中提供低延迟 early-warning\\\"\\n      - \\\"拓扑方法捕捉表示空间的各向异性和多尺度结构丧失\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"topology-based monitoring 是与 spectral 方法正交的 collapse 检测路线。我们排除了该方法（计算开销高、可解释性弱），但 related work 需要对比说明选择 spectral/calibration panel 而非 topology 的理由\\\"\\n    gaps: \\\"计算开销显著高于 effective rank 等标量指标；未在 iterative synthetic training loop 场景下测试；与 ECE 等轻量 canary 无对比\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"Collapse Index\\\", \\\"persistent homology features\\\"]\\n\\n  - id: \\\"bergsma2026scaling\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Scaling with Collapse: Efficient and Predictable Training of LLM Families\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Bergsma et al.\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv (2509.25087, rev. Mar 2026)\\\"\\n    year: 2026\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25087\\\"\\n    category: \\\"canary-metrics\\\"\\n    role: \\\"background\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"'Collapse' 此处指 loss curve 归一化后坍缩到通用轨迹（非 model collapse）\\\"\\n      - \\\"deviation-from-collapse 作为训练病理的早期诊断信号\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"不同语境下的 'collapse'。但其 '偏离通用轨迹 = 训练出问题' 的监控思路与我们的 canary 理念相似——均从时序异常检测角度切入\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"loss curve deviation\\\", \\\"scaling law exponents\\\"]\\n\\n  # ============================================================\\n  # DIVERSITY METRICS (BACKGROUND)\\n  # ============================================================\\n  - id: \\\"zhu2018texygen\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Texygen: A Benchmarking Platform for Text Generation Models\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Yaoming Zhu et al.\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"SIGIR 2018\\\"\\n    year: 2018\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/1802.01886\\\"\\n    category: \\\"canary-metrics\\\"\\n    role: \\\"background\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"提出 Self-BLEU 度量：计算生成句子间的 BLEU 相似度，Self-BLEU 越高多样性越低\\\"\\n      - \\\"成为文本生成多样性评估的标准基准之一\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"Self-BLEU 是我们 diversity canary 的经典候选\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"Self-BLEU\\\", \\\"BLEU\\\"]\\n\\n  - id: \\\"li2016diversity\\\"\\n    title: \\\"A Diversity-Promoting Objective Function for Neural Conversation Models\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Jiwei Li et al.\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"NAACL 2016\\\"\\n    year: 2016\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/1510.03055\\\"\\n    category: \\\"canary-metrics\\\"\\n    role: \\\"background\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"提出 distinct-1 / distinct-2 指标衡量生成文本的 n-gram 多样性\\\"\\n      - \\\"计算 unique n-gram 数占总 n-gram 数的比例\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"distinct-n 是最廉价的 diversity canary 之一，计算开销极低\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"distinct-1\\\", \\\"distinct-2\\\"]\\n\\n  # ============================================================\\n  # REPRESENTATION / SPECTRAL COLLAPSE\\n  # ============================================================\\n  - id: \\\"jing2022understanding\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Understanding Dimensional Collapse in Contrastive Self-supervised Learning\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Li Jing et al.\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"ICLR 2022\\\"\\n    year: 2022\\n    url: \\\"https://openreview.net/forum?id=YevsQ05DEN7\\\"\\n    category: \\\"canary-metrics\\\"\\n    role: \\\"method-component\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"定义 dimensional collapse：embedding 向量只跨越低维子空间\\\"\\n      - \\\"effective rank（基于归一化特征值 entropy 的指数）作为 collapse 度量\\\"\\n      - \\\"strong augmentation 和 implicit regularization 驱动低秩解\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"effective rank 的定义和计算方式可直接迁移到我们的 embedding 空间监控\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"effective rank\\\", \\\"singular value spectrum\\\"]\\n\\n  - id: \\\"noci2024mind\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Mind the Gap: A Spectral Analysis of Rank Collapse and Signal Propagation in Transformers\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Lorenzo Noci et al.\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"NeurIPS 2024\\\"\\n    year: 2024\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07799\\\"\\n    category: \\\"canary-metrics\\\"\\n    role: \\\"method-component\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"用 Random Matrix Theory 分析 attention 层的 rank collapse\\\"\\n      - \\\"发现 attention matrix 最大两个奇异值之间的 spectral gap 是 width-collapse 的根因\\\"\\n      - \\\"rank collapse in depth: 所有 token 沿层收敛到单一表示\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"spectral gap 作为另一种 spectral canary 候选；其 RMT 分析框架可启发我们的理论工作\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"spectral gap\\\", \\\"singular values\\\", \\\"effective rank\\\"]\\n\\n  # ============================================================\\n  # DATA MIXING & ACCUMULATION\\n  # ============================================================\\n  - id: \\\"kazdan2025collapse\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Collapse or Thrive? Perils and Promises of Synthetic Data in a Self-Generating World\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Joshua Kazdan, Rylan Schaeffer, Apratim Dey, Matthias Gerstgrasser, Rafael Rafailov, David L. Donoho, Sanmi Koyejo\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"ICML 2025\\\"\\n    year: 2025\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.16713\\\"\\n    category: \\\"data-mixing\\\"\\n    role: \\\"foundation\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"系统对比 replace vs accumulate 场景\\\"\\n      - \\\"replace → collapse；accumulate → 可避免 collapse\\\"\\n      - \\\"发现 real data 和 synthetic data 之间存在非平凡的交互效应\\\"\\n      - \\\"synthetic data 的价值取决于 real data 的绝对数量\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"直接对应我们的 alpha（mixing ratio）实验维度；其 replace vs accumulate 框架是我们实验设计的重要参考\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"test loss\\\"]\\n\\n  - id: \\\"dey2024breaking\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Is Model Collapse Inevitable? Breaking the Curse of Recursion by Accumulating Real and Synthetic Data\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Apratim Dey, David Donoho, et al.\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"ICML 2024\\\"\\n    year: 2024\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.01413\\\"\\n    category: \\\"data-mixing\\\"\\n    role: \\\"foundation\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"替换数据 → 发散 population risk；累积数据 → 避免发散\\\"\\n      - \\\"证明累积策略在多种模型（GMM, HMM, LLM）上有效\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"为我们的 alpha 实验提供理论预测：累积 vs 替换是 collapse 速率的关键调节因子\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"population risk\\\"]\\n\\n  - id: \\\"epstein2024howbad\\\"\\n    title: \\\"How Bad is Training on Synthetic Data? A Statistical Analysis of Language Model Collapse\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Mohamed El Amine Epstein et al.\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv (2404.05090)\\\"\\n    year: 2024\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.05090\\\"\\n    category: \\\"data-mixing\\\"\\n    role: \\\"background\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"纯合成数据训练不可避免 collapse\\\"\\n      - \\\"给出 synthetic data 比例上界的估计——低于此比例可避免 collapse\\\"\\n      - \\\"递归训练使原始分布尾部消失\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"其 mixing ratio 上界估计可对比我们实验中观测到的 canary 触发阈值\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"perplexity\\\"]\\n\\n  - id: \\\"fu2025selfconsuming\\\"\\n    title: \\\"A Theoretical Perspective: How to Prevent Model Collapse in Self-consuming Training Loops\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Shi Fu, Yingjie Wang, Yuzhu Chen, Xinmei Tian, Dacheng Tao\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"ICLR 2025\\\"\\n    year: 2025\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.18865\\\"\\n    category: \\\"data-mixing\\\"\\n    role: \\\"theoretical-foundation\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"引入 recursive stability 概念，首次给出 self-consuming loop 的泛化分析\\\"\\n      - \\\"恒定比例的真实数据即可保证 STL 收敛；同时给出最优合成数据量的 insight\\\"\\n      - \\\"模型架构和 real/synthetic 数据比例共同决定 STL 的成败\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"为 post-MVP α-sweep（synthetic ratio 梯度）实验提供理论预期：50% accumulation 条件下收敛有理论保证\\\"\\n    gaps: \\\"理论分析限于特定 generalization bound 框架，未涉及 canary 指标的行为；未讨论不同 α 下 collapse 速率的精细结构\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"generalization bound\\\", \\\"population risk\\\"]\\n\\n  - id: \\\"bakshi2026improvement\\\"\\n    title: \\\"From Collapse to Improvement: Statistical Perspectives on the Evolutionary Dynamics of Iterative Training on Contaminated Sources\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Soham Bakshi, Sunrit Chakraborty\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv (2602.10531)\\\"\\n    year: 2026\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10531\\\"\\n    category: \\\"data-mixing\\\"\\n    role: \\\"theoretical-foundation\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"αt（每轮混合权重）trajectory 和 nt（每轮样本量）scaling 的交互决定三种 regime：collapse / improvement / consistency\\\"\\n      - \\\"当 αt 衰减足够快且 nt 增长足够快时，迭代估计可收敛到真实分布（improvement regime）\\\"\\n      - \\\"αt 衰减不足或 nt 增长不足则进入 collapse regime；边界条件给出 consistency regime\\\"\\n      - \\\"在 next-token prediction 语言模型上分析完整迭代演化动态\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"αt/nt 条件直接对应我们 α-sweep 实验的理论预期：不同 synthetic ratio 衰减曲线下 canary 应呈现不同时序模式\\\"\\n    gaps: \\\"理论结果基于渐近分析，有限代数下的 transient behavior 未充分刻画；未讨论哪些指标能区分三种 regime\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"population risk\\\", \\\"total variation distance\\\"]\\n\\n  - id: \\\"he2025golden\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Golden Ratio Weighting Prevents Model Collapse\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Shiliang He, Zhongbin Xu, Yan Cheng\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv (2502.18049)\\\"\\n    year: 2025\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.18049\\\"\\n    category: \\\"data-mixing\\\"\\n    role: \\\"theoretical-foundation\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"最优 real data 权重在 Gaussian estimation、GLM、非参数估计中均收敛到黄金比例倒数 1/φ ≈ 0.618\\\"\\n      - \\\"最优权重 w*(λ) 作为正则化参数的函数既非单调也非凸——risk landscape 在 mixing ratio 空间上本身不是单调的\\\"\\n      - \\\"提供了 mixing ratio 非单调性的首个理论框架\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"我们在 α-sweep 中观测到的非单调 dose-response（α=0.25 比 α=0.50 collapse 更严重）可视为该理论在 diversity metrics 上的实证验证。论文 Discussion 的理论锚点。\\\"\\n    gaps: \\\"理论基于线性回归和高斯分布，未直接推广到 LLM diversity metrics\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"population risk\\\", \\\"optimal weight\\\"]\\n\\n  - id: \\\"xu2025mixing\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Preventing Model Collapse Under Overparametrization: Optimal Mixing Ratios\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Zhongbin Xu, Yan Cheng\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv (2509.22341)\\\"\\n    year: 2025\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22341\\\"\\n    category: \\\"data-mixing\\\"\\n    role: \\\"theoretical-foundation\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"过参数化 regime（插值学习 + ridge 回归）中分析最优混合比例\\\"\\n      - \\\"插值学习：最优 real data 比例收敛到 1/φ\\\"\\n      - \\\"Ridge 回归：最优比例受谱几何控制，至少为 1/2\\\"\\n      - \\\"w > 1/3 时 variance 随迭代单调递减；w < 1/3 时 variance 可能增长——存在临界阈值\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"1/3 临界阈值与我们 α=0.25（real weight=0.75 vs synthetic weight=0.25）处出现最强 collapse 的观测有概念对应。不同 regime 下 variance 行为质变支持我们的 regime-dependent 叙事。\\\"\\n    gaps: \\\"参数空间不完全对齐（他们的 w 是 real data weight，我们的 α 是 synthetic ratio）；未讨论 diversity metrics\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"variance\\\", \\\"population risk\\\", \\\"optimal mixing ratio\\\"]\\n\\n  # ============================================================\\n  # MITIGATION & VERIFICATION\\n  # ============================================================\\n  - id: \\\"feng2025escaping\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Escaping Model Collapse via Synthetic Data Verification\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Yunzhen Feng et al.\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"NeurIPS 2025\\\"\\n    year: 2025\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.16657\\\"\\n    category: \\\"data-mixing\\\"\\n    role: \\\"background\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"通过外部 verifier（human 或 better model）注入信息可避免 collapse\\\"\\n      - \\\"verifier-guided retraining 带来短期改善，但最终收敛到 verifier 的 knowledge center\\\"\\n      - \\\"除非 verifier 完全可靠，早期收益会 plateau 甚至逆转\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"verification 作为 intervention 策略——如果 canary 触发，可考虑此类 reactive intervention\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"test loss\\\"]\\n\\n  - id: \\\"barzilai2025consistency\\\"\\n    title: \\\"When Models Don't Collapse: On the Consistency of Iterative MLE\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Daniel Barzilai, Ohad Shamir\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"ICML 2025\\\"\\n    year: 2025\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19046\\\"\\n    category: \\\"core-collapse\\\"\\n    role: \\\"background\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"在标准 MLE 一致性假设下，即使 real data fraction 趋近零也可避免 collapse\\\"\\n      - \\\"但证明了某些情况下 collapse 可以任意快地发生——首次给出严格 collapse 反例\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"理论上的 collapse 可发生任意快的结论强化了 early warning 的价值\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: []\\n\\n  - id: \\\"dces2026counter\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Countering Model Collapse in Iterative Self-Training via Dynamic Center-Edge Sampling\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Anonymous\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"MDPI Electronics (2026)\\\"\\n    year: 2026\\n    url: \\\"https://www.mdpi.com/2079-9292/15/4/869\\\"\\n    category: \\\"data-mixing\\\"\\n    role: \\\"background\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"提出 DCES（dynamic center-edge sampling）数据选择框架\\\"\\n      - \\\"DCES 在 ECE（0.1058 vs 0.1200）和 entropy 保持上优于随机选择\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"其 ECE 和 entropy 对比数据为我们的指标 baseline 提供参考\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"ECE\\\", \\\"entropy\\\"]\\n\\n  # ============================================================\\n  # POSITION / SURVEY / EPISTEMICS\\n  # ============================================================\\n  - id: \\\"schaeffer2025position\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Position: Model Collapse Does Not Mean What You Think\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Rylan Schaeffer, Joshua Kazdan, Alvan Caleb Arulandu, Sanmi Koyejo\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv (2503.03150)\\\"\\n    year: 2025\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.03150\\\"\\n    category: \\\"core-collapse\\\"\\n    role: \\\"background\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"文献中实际包含 8 种不同且有时矛盾的 model collapse 定义\\\"\\n      - \\\"许多 collapse 预测依赖于与现实不符的假设\\\"\\n      - \\\"多种 collapse 场景是可以轻易避免的\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"提醒我们明确定义 collapse 的操作标准；不同定义可能需要不同的 canary 指标\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: []\\n\\n  - id: \\\"wang2025epistemic\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Epistemic Diversity and Knowledge Collapse in Large Language Models\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Wang et al.\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv (2510.04226)\\\"\\n    year: 2025\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04226\\\"\\n    category: \\\"core-collapse\\\"\\n    role: \\\"background\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"27 个 LLM 在 155 个主题上的 epistemic diversity 评估\\\"\\n      - \\\"所有模型的 epistemic diversity 低于基本 web 搜索\\\"\\n      - \\\"模型越大 epistemic diversity 反而越低；RAG 有正面效果\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"knowledge collapse 维度可扩展我们对 diversity canary 的理解——不仅是 lexical/syntactic 还有 epistemic\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: [\\\"epistemic diversity\\\"]\\n\\n  # ============================================================\\n  # METHODOLOGY (GRANGER / LEAD-LAG)\\n  # ============================================================\\n  - id: \\\"shojaie2022granger\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Granger Causality: A Review and Recent Advances\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Ali Shojaie, Emily B. Fox\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"Annual Review of Statistics and Its Application\\\"\\n    year: 2022\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2105.02675\\\"\\n    category: \\\"methodology\\\"\\n    role: \\\"method-component\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"Granger causality 综述：从经典线性到非线性/neural 方法\\\"\\n      - \\\"Panel data 场景的 Granger causality 扩展\\\"\\n      - \\\"VAR 和 neural Granger causality 的对比\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"为我们的 panel Granger causality 分析提供方法论基础\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: []\\n\\n  - id: \\\"marcinkevics2022neural\\\"\\n    title: \\\"Granger Causality Using Neural Networks\\\"\\n    authors: \\\"Raphael Marcinkevics, Julia Vogt\\\"\\n    venue: \\\"arXiv (2208.03703)\\\"\\n    year: 2022\\n    url: \\\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2208.03703\\\"\\n    category: \\\"methodology\\\"\\n    role: \\\"method-component\\\"\\n    key_findings:\\n      - \\\"用 neural network 估计 Granger causality，突破线性假设\\\"\\n      - \\\"两个模型对比法：含/不含外生变量的预测误差差异即 Granger causality 证据\\\"\\n    relevance_to_us: \\\"我们的多指标时序可能存在非线性关系，neural Granger causality 是自然选择\\\"\\n    metrics_studied: []\\n\\ngaps:\\n  - \\\"无人系统做过 canary 指标的 lead-lag / Granger causality 分析——现有工作只在事后观察到指标变化，未量化哪些指标能提前多少步预测 collapse\\\"\\n  - \\\"ECE 在 collapse 场景下几乎未被系统研究——chen2025scales 是唯一报告 ECE 先于 accuracy 退化的工作，但未做因果分析\\\"\\n  - \\\"embedding effective rank 未在 iterative synthetic training 场景下被测试——仅在 contrastive learning/transformer 训练中研究过\\\"\\n  - \\\"多指标 panel 框架缺失——无人同时追踪 ECE + effective rank + diversity + perplexity 的时序行为并分析其 lead-lag 关系\\\"\\n  - \\\"alpha（synthetic ratio）对 canary 灵敏度的影响未被研究——现有工作只关注 alpha 对最终性能的影响，不关注 canary 的 lead time 如何随 alpha 变化\\\"\\n  - \\\"diversity dose-response 非单调性（α=0.25 比 α=0.50 collapse 更严重）无先例报告——He et al. 2025 从理论上预测 risk landscape 非单调，但无人在 LLM diversity 上实证验证\\\"\\n  - \\\"predictive intervention vs reactive intervention 缺少对比——没有工作量化 early warning 带来的实际干预收益\\\"\\n  - \\\"surplexity 和 spectral alignment 未在 iterative collapse 场景下交叉验证\\\"\\n  - \\\"跨任务 canary 普适性未知——reasoning vs retrieval vs creative tasks 中哪些 canary 更可靠\\\"\\n\\nnovelty_assessment: |\\n  我们项目的核心 novelty 在于：\\n\\n  1. **Panel Granger Causality 框架**：首次将多指标时序数据的因果分析方法应用于 model collapse 早期预警。\\n     现有工作（chen2025scales, gambetta2024surplexity）只是事后观察到某些指标先变化，\\n     但没有人做过系统的 lead-lag 量化分析。\\n\\n  2. **多指标 Canary Panel**：同时追踪 ECE、effective rank、Self-BLEU/distinct-n、\\n     perplexity、entropy、surplexity 等指标，分析其相互关系和各自的 lead time。\\n     现有工作都是单一指标或最多两三个指标的孤立分析。\\n\\n  3. **alpha-dependent Canary Sensitivity**：系统研究 synthetic ratio 如何影响各 canary\\n     指标的灵敏度和 lead time。dohmatob2025strong 证明极小的 alpha 就能触发 collapse，\\n     但没人研究 canary 在不同 alpha 下的行为。\\n\\n  4. **与已有工作的差异**：\\n     - vs chen2025scales: 他们发现 ECE 先退化但没有做因果分析；我们用 Granger causality 量化\\n     - vs gambetta2024surplexity: 他们提出 surplexity 用于检测和缓解 collapse；\\n       我们将其纳入更广的 panel 并研究其 lead-lag 特性\\n     - vs he2025closer: 他们在 diffusion 中用 entropy 作为指标；我们在 LLM 中测试更多指标\\n     - vs qiu2025spectral: 他们的 SA 用于训练 loss explosion 预警；\\n       我们将类似思路迁移到 iterative collapse 场景\\n\\n  5. **潜在风险**：chen2025scales 已经非常接近我们的方向。如果他们扩展到 lead-lag 分析，\\n     我们的 novelty 会被削弱。需要快速推进并在方法论（Granger causality）上建立壁垒。\\n\", \"total_chars\": 29202, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"

```text
Now I have all the information I need. Let me synthesize the alignment check report.

Let me organize what I've learned:

**Experiments Status:**

DONE (successful):
- MVP: rep_42, rep_43, rep_44, rep_45 (α=1.0, 4 seeds) ✓
- method_ablation_phase1 ✓
- plan_009_bootstrap_ty ✓
- alpha_sweep_a000_s42 (α=0.0 control) ✓
- alpha_sweep_a025_s42_v3 (α=0.25, seed=42) ✓


- alpha_sweep_a050_s42 (α=0.50, seed=42) ✓

Two experiments are currently running: the α=0.75 sweep is partway through at generation 2 of 10 with about 3.5 hours remaining, and plan_006 is further along at generation 14 of 20 with roughly 3 hours left. I've got several more alpha sweeps queued up to test additional seeds and parameter values once these finish.

I'm also preparing a second seed run for the 20-generation plan and setting up a finer grid around α=0.60 to better characterize the transition region. Now I'm examining the evidence for the canary-to-diversity Granger-causal relationship. The α=1.0 condition shows strong support across all four seeds with perfect FDR significance and consistent cross-correlation lags, but this only holds at that extreme value. At α=0.50 and α=0.25, the strict statistical tests fail completely, so the real question is whether α=0.75 will show intermediate behavior or a sharp threshold effect.

For the second claim about regime-dependent predictability, I'm seeing a clear dose-response pattern in the diversity metric across the α sweep, though there's an interesting non-monotonicity between α=0.25 and α=0.50 that I need to verify with more seeds. The detectability gap is stark—α=1.0 achieves perfect statistical significance while α=0.50 shows nothing, and the perplexity channel is confirming the underlying mechanism.

Now looking at the third claim on method interactions, the naive approach dominates with 16/16 detections versus only 4/16 for the more sophisticated method, and the spurious rate jumps dramatically depending on the configuration. The differencing analysis reveals something important: the diversity channel is robust to this transformation while the perplexity channel completely breaks down. The d_max parameter is a critical control point—varying it swings the spurious rate from 0% to 94%—and at α=0.50 specifically, the naive cross-correlation catches all 8 cases while the stricter methods find nothing.

For the literature side, I need to add Scheffer et al. 2009/2012 to the map since it's a required citation, though the other key papers—He et al. on golden ratios, Xu & Cheng on mixing ratios, and Truong & Truong's theoretical work—are already properly documented and linked to their relevant findings. Both citations are properly anchored to the non-monotonic dose-response pattern we observed. Now I'm synthesizing the key findings: Claim 1 has strong evidence at α=1.0 but needs the α=0.75 results to fill the gap, Claim 2 shows a clear detectability boundary though the single-seed non-monotonic case is a weak point, and Claim 3 is the most novel and well-supported. Let me draft the report with these priorities in mind.

The main vulnerabilities are the single-seed non-monotonic result, the critical dependence on α=0.75 for establishing practical relevance, and the fact that we're only working with Pythia-410M on WikiText-103 so far. The multi-seed experiments for α=0.25 and α=0.50 are queued but haven't started yet. The pending experiments will directly address these gaps: α=0.75 strengthens both Claim 1's scope and Claim 2's precision, plan_006 tests long-run stability and helps with the ECE decision, and the multi-seed runs will finally give us confidence intervals for the non-monotonic pattern. I should also note that Scheffer et al. is missing from the literature map.

I'm checking the evidence hierarchy—Claim 3 is strongest, then Claim 2, then Claim 1—and making sure the paper gives Claim 3 the prominence it deserves. The framing in paper_framing.md looks appropriately calibrated to the actual evidence strength, so the hedging feels right.

  "content": "# Framing-Evidence Alignment Check (2026-05-15)\n\n## 1. 各 Claim 证据覆盖率\n\n### Claim 1: Canary→Diversity Granger-Causal Precedence — **中**\n\n| 证据维度 | 状态 | 详情 |\n|----------|------|------|\n| α=1.0, 4 seeds | **完成** | 24/24 FDR-sig, cross-corr lag=-1 (std=0.0), permutation Granger 差分后 4/4 SIG |\n| α=0.50 | **完成** | 0/4 FDR-sig（严格方法全归零），仅 naive_xcorr 8/8 |\n| α=0.25 | **完成** | 类似 α=0.50，严格方法不过 FDR |\n| α=0.75 | **在跑** | gen 2/10, ETA ~3.5h。**Pivotal**: 决定 scope 是 \"α≥0.75\" 还是 \"α=1.0 only\" |\n| plan_006 (T=21) | **在跑** | gen 14/20, ETA ~3h。TY power 改善 + ECE co-primary 判定 |\n| 多架构验证 | **未做** | plan_010 (Pythia-1.4B / GPT-2) 仅 PREPARING |\n\n**评估**: α=1.0 证据坚实，但 scope 极窄。当前措辞 \"at high contamination levels\" 是审慎的，但若 α=0.75 也归零，\"high\" 实际上只意味着 \"full replacement\"——实用价值论述将非常困难。paper_framing.md 约束 #21 的措辞已正确预判了这一风险。\n\n**当前最大缺口**: α=0.75 结果（数小时内出）。\n\n---\n\n### Claim 2: Regime-Dependent Predictability — **中偏弱**\n\n| 证据维度 | 状态 | 详情 |\n|----------|------|------|\n| Drift dose-response (5 α) | **完成** | d1: -13.8%@α=0 → -40.3%@α=0.25 → -37.3%@α=0.50 → ~-93%@α=1.0 |\n| 非单调性 (α=0.25 > α=0.50) | **单 seed** | 仅 seed=42，差值 3pp (40.3% vs 37.3%)，无置信区间 |\n| Detectability boundary | **完成** | α=1.0 24/24 vs α=0.50 0/4 FDR-sig，清晰二元分界 |\n| Perplexity 伪回归确认 | **完成** | D017: 差分后 0/8 SIG (F≈0, p=0.99) |\n| ECE regime switch | **单 seed** | α=0.25 gen1 下降 -14%（反向）；α=1.0 gen1 +2926% |\n| Multi-seed (α=0.25/0.50) | **QUEUED** | 4 runs 排队中，未开始 |\n\n**评估**: Detectability boundary 和 perplexity 伪回归是稳固的子发现。但非单调 dose-response（Claim 2 最吸引眼球的部分）仅有 3pp 差值、单 seed，统计上不可区分于噪声。paper_framing.md 约束 #23 已正确要求标注 \"observed at seed=42; multi-seed confirmation required\"，但主文若要将此作为 regime map 的 \"核心特征\"（约束 #22），multi-seed 是刚需。\n\n**当前最大缺口**: multi-seed 尚未开跑。这是 Weak Accept → Accept 的首要条件（约束 #23 原话）。\n\n---\n\n### Claim 3: Method × Regime Interaction — **强**\n\n| 证据维度 | 状态 | 详情 |\n|----------|------|------|\n| Method ablation (5 methods) | **完成** | naive 16/16 → TY 4/16; spurious_rate 75%/62.5% |\n| 2×2 差分对比表 | **完成** | diversity 通道不变 (12/12→12/12)，perplexity 通道归零 (6-8/8→0/8) |\n| d_max sensitivity | **完成** | 0%↔94% spurious rate 震荡 |\n| TY power analysis | **完成** | T=10 power 0.05-0.20 (plan_009, N=1000 Monte Carlo) |\n| Method×regime 极端例证 | **完成** | α=0.50: naive_xcorr 100% sig vs strict 0% sig |\n| BH FDR 校正 | **完成** | 全 battery 已加 FDR，无结论翻转 |\n\n**评估**: 多层证据结构完整（Layer 1-3），无需额外实验即可成立。最强且最 novel 的 claim。paper_framing.md 约束 #25 正确识别了证据强度排序 (Claim 3 > 2 > 1)。\n\n**无重大缺口。**\n\n---\n\n## 2. 当前最大风险点（按严重性排序）\n\n1. **Multi-seed 全部 QUEUED（未跑）** — 非单调 dose-response 和 ECE regime switch 均为 point estimate。若 multi-seed 推翻非单调性，Claim 2 的 regime map 叙事需要大幅修改。这是唯一可能迫使论文结构性改写的风险。\n\n2. **α=0.75 结果（数小时内）** — 若也 null，Claim 1 scope 收缩至 \"α=1.0 only\"。论文标题/摘要中 \"canary prediction\" 的实用价值论述将依赖 pure boundary mapping 定位，reviewer 可能质疑 \"so what\"。\n\n3. **单模型 + 单数据集** — Pythia-410M × WikiText-103。plan_010（Pythia-1.4B/GPT-2）和 domain-transfer（C4）均未启动。Reviewer concern 表中已列出但无应对实验。这是 venue-specific 风险——AAAI 对 empirical breadth 容忍度稍高，但仍需至少 scale spot-check。\n\n4. **Scheffer et al. 未进 literature_map.yaml** — paper_framing.md 约束 #13 标注为 \"必引\"，Story Spine 中已提及，但 literature_map.yaml 中缺失。需补录。\n\n---\n\n## 3. 在跑/待跑实验对各 Claim 的贡献\n\n| 实验 | 状态 | Claim 1 | Claim 2 | Claim 3 | 说明 |\n|------|------|---------|---------|---------|------|\n| **α=0.75** | RUNNING (2/10) | **核心** | 高 | 中 | Claim 1 scope 扩展的唯一机会；Claim 2 regime boundary 精度提升 |\n| **plan_006** (20gen) | RUNNING (14/20) | 高 | 中 | 中 | T=21 提升 TY power（Claim 1 补充）；ECE co-primary 判定；长序列 diversity lead 稳定性 |\n| **multi-seed** (4 runs) | QUEUED | 低 | **核心** | 低 | 非单调性置信区间 = Claim 2 的生死线；ECE regime switch 可重复性 |\n\n优先级建议：multi-seed（a025_s43 优先）应在 α=0.75 完成后立即上 GPU。\n\n---\n\n## 4. Framing 措辞与证据强度匹配检查\n\n### 措辞已恰当的部分\n- Claim 1 主句限定 \"at high contamination levels\" — 与当前仅 α=1.0 稳固的证据匹配 ✓\n- Perplexity 通道用 \"spurious association confirmed by first-differencing\" — 措辞精确 ✓\n- \"Regime map\" 而非 \"phase diagram\" — 4-5 个 α 值不足以用物理学术语 ✓\n- CRS 降级为 illustration — 与 Dream 三档共识一致 ✓\n- TY 降级为辅助指标 — 有 plan_009 power analysis 支撑 ✓\n- f² 效应量禁用 Cohen 基准 — 正确处理小 df_resid 问题 ✓\n\n### 需要微调的措辞\n\n1. **Headline result template 中 \"1-2 generations ahead\"** — 当前仅 α=1.0 有此 lead，α=0.25/0.50 无显著 lead。建议加限定：\"at full synthetic replacement (α=1.0), token entropy exhibits ... 1-2 generations ahead\"。\n\n2. **Story Spine 中 \"predictability regime map delineates which collapse dimensions are forecastable\"** — 当前 regime map 只有 2 个非平凡点确认有/无 Granger sig（α=1.0 有, α=0.50 无），α=0.75 pending。若 α=0.75 null，map 退化为单点。建议加 \"preliminary\" 或明确说 \"identifies a detectability boundary between α=0.50 and α=1.0\"，而非暗示连续 mapping。\n\n3. **非单调 dose-response 在 Evidence strategy 中列为 \"Primary = raw drift magnitude dose-response\"** — 这是正确策略（方法无关），但主文中非单调性结论需显著标注单 seed 限制。当前约束 #23 已要求标注，但 Headline result template 中未提及非单调性，建议保持如此——在 multi-seed 确认前不放入 headline。\n\n4. **ECE \"conditional secondary canary\" 措辞** — paper_framing.md 说 \"secondary only in TY consistency; equivalent across all other methods\"。但约束 #20 揭示 ECE 在低 α 有反向信号。建议措辞更新为 \"conditional secondary: equivalent to token_entropy at α≥0.50 across non-TY methods, but unreliable at α=0.25 due to first-generation sign reversal\"。\n\n### 文献 contextualization 缺口\n\n| 文献 | Claim | 状态 |\n|------|-------|------|\n| Scheffer et al. 2009/2012 | Story Spine | paper_framing.md 已标注必引，**但 literature_map.yaml 中缺失** — 需补录 |\n| He et al. 2025 (golden ratio) | Claim 2 非单调性 | literature_map.yaml 已锚定 ✓ |\n| Xu & Cheng 2025 (mixing ratios) | Claim 2 regime boundary | literature_map.yaml 已锚定 ✓ |\n| Truong & Truong 2025 | Claim 2 (canary 失效边界) | literature_map.yaml 已收录 ✓ |\n| Chen et al. 2025 (scales) | Claim 1 (ECE 先驱) | literature_map.yaml 已收录，4 点差异化已写入 Story Spine ✓ |\n\n---\n\n## 5. Reviewer 攻击面预判\n\n按杀伤力排序：\n\n1. **\"Only works at α=1.0, who trains on 100% synthetic data?\"** — 若 α=0.75 null，这是致命攻击。应对：boundary mapping 贡献定位 + 引用 Dohmatob 2025 \"即使极小比例也触发 collapse\" 论证研究必要性。但 reviewer 可能不买账。\n\n2. **\"Single model, single dataset\"** — 无应对实验。plan_010 和 domain-transfer 均未启动。至少需要 Pythia-1.4B spot-check 一个核心条件。\n\n3. **\"Non-monotonic dose-response is noise\"** — 3pp 差值单 seed，reviewer 一句 \"within noise margin\" 即可驳回。multi-seed 是唯一解。\n\n4. **\"Granger ≠ causality, why not do intervention?\"** — paper_framing.md 已准备 Discussion 声明 + future work。CRS illustration 部分缓解但不消除。风险可控。\n\n5. **\"T=10 too short for any time-series inference\"** — plan_006 (T=21) 在跑，plan_009 已提供 power analysis。若 plan_006 完成且结论一致，此攻击面大幅缩小。\n"
}

equivalent across all other methods\"。但约束 #20 揭示 ECE 在低 α 有反向信号。建议措辞更新为 \"conditional secondary: equivalent to token_entropy at α≥0.50 across non-TY methods, but unreliable at α=0.25 due to first-generation sign reversal\"。\n\n### 文献 contextualization 缺口\n\n| 文献 | Claim | 状态 |\n|------|-------|------|\n| Scheffer et al. 2009/2012 | Story Spine | paper_framing.md 已标注必引，**但 literature_map.yaml 中缺失** — 需补录 |\n| He et al. 2025 (golden ratio) | Claim 2 非单调性 | literature_map.yaml 已锚定 ✓ |\n| Xu & Cheng 2025 (mixing ratios) | Claim 2 regime boundary | literature_map.yaml 已锚定 ✓ |\n| Truong & Truong 2025 | Claim 2 (canary 失效边界) | literature_map.yaml 已收录 ✓ |\n| Chen et al. 2025 (scales) | Claim 1 (ECE 先驱) | literature_map.yaml 已收录，4 点差异化已写入 Story Spine ✓ |\n\n---\n\n## 5. Reviewer 攻击面预判\n\n按杀伤力排序：\n\n1. **\"Only works at α=1.0, who trains on 100% synthetic data?\"** — 若 α=0.75 null，这是致命攻击。应对：boundary mapping 贡献定位 + 引用 Dohmatob 2025 \"即使极小比例也触发 collapse\" 论证研究必要性。但 reviewer 可能不买账。\n\n2. **\"Single model, single dataset\"** — 无应对实验。plan_010 和 domain-transfer 均未启动。至少需要 Pythia-1.4B spot-check 一个核心条件。\n\n3. **\"Non-monotonic dose-response is noise\"** — 3pp 差值单 seed，reviewer 一句 \"within noise margin\" 即可驳回。multi-seed 是唯一解。\n\n4. **\"Granger ≠ causality, why not do intervention?\"** — paper_framing.md 已准备 Discussion 声明 + future work。CRS illustration 部分缓解但不消除。风险可控。\n\n5. **\"T=10 too short for any time-series inference\"** — plan_006 (T=21) 在跑，plan_009 已提供 power analysis。若 plan_006 完成且结论一致，此攻击面大幅缩小。\n",

报告已写入 `artifacts/framing_alignment_check.md` 并注册。

核心结论：

- **Claim 3（method sensitivity）证据最强**，无重大缺口
- **Claim 1（Granger precedence）证据在 α=1.0 坚实但 scope 极窄**，α=0.75（在跑，数小时内出）是 scope 扩展的唯一机会
- **Claim 2（regime-dependent）最薄弱**——非单调 dose-response 仅 3pp 差值、单 seed，multi-seed 全部 QUEUED 未跑，这是 Weak Accept → Accept 的首要瓶颈
- **literature_map.yaml 缺 Scheffer et al. 2009/2012**，paper_framing.md 约束 #13 标注为必引但未收录
- Headline result template 和 regime map 措辞需要加限定词以匹配当前证据强度
